===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.run()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 341-379
Documentation:
/**
   * Runs the Compiler and calls System.exit() with the exit status of the
   * compiler.
   */
Method code:
341 |   final public void run() {
342 |     int result = 0;
343 |     int runs = 1;
344 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
345 |       runs = NUM_RUNS_TO_DETERMINE_OPTIMAL_ORDER;
346 |       PhaseOptimizer.randomizeLoops();
347 |     }
348 |     try {
349 |       for (int i = 0; i < runs && result == 0; i++) {
350 |         runTimeStats.recordStartRun();
351 |         result = doRun();
352 |         runTimeStats.recordEndRun();
353 |       }
354 |     } catch (AbstractCommandLineRunner.FlagUsageException e) {
355 |       System.err.println(e.getMessage());
356 |       result = -1;
357 |     } catch (Throwable t) {
358 |       t.printStackTrace();
359 |       result = -2;
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
363 |       runTimeStats.outputBestPhaseOrdering();
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     try {
367 |       if (jsOutput instanceof Closeable) {
368 |         ((Closeable) jsOutput).close();
369 |       }
370 |     } catch (IOException e) {
371 |       throw Throwables.propagate(e);
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     if (testMode) {
375 |       exitCodeReceiverForTesting.apply(result);
376 |     } else {
377 |       System.exit(result);
378 |     }
379 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Token.name(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.getLegacyOutputCharset()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 990-999
Documentation:
/**
   * Query the flag for the output charset.
   *
   * Let the outputCharset be the same as the input charset... except if
   * we're reading in UTF-8 by default.  By tradition, we've always
   * output ASCII to avoid various hiccups with different browsers,
   * proxies and firewalls.
   *
   * @return Name of the charset to use when writing outputs. Guaranteed to
   *    be a supported charset.
   * @throws FlagUsageException if flag is not a valid Charset name.
   */
Method code:
990 |   private String getLegacyOutputCharset() throws FlagUsageException {
991 |     if (!config.charset.isEmpty()) {
992 |       if (!Charset.isSupported(config.charset)) {
993 |         throw new FlagUsageException(config.charset +
994 |             " is not a valid charset name.");
995 |       }
996 |       return config.charset;
997 |     }
998 |     return "US-ASCII";
999 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.writeOutput(Appendable,Compiler,String,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 651-682
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes code to an output stream, optionally wrapping it in an arbitrary
   * wrapper that contains a placeholder where the code should be inserted.
   */
Method code:
651 |   static void writeOutput(Appendable out, Compiler compiler, String code,
652 |       String wrapper, String codePlaceholder) throws IOException {
653 |     int pos = wrapper.indexOf(codePlaceholder);
654 |     if (pos != -1) {
655 |       String prefix = "";
656 | 
657 |       if (pos > 0) {
658 |         prefix = wrapper.substring(0, pos);
659 |         out.append(prefix);
660 |       }
661 | 
662 |       out.append(code);
663 | 
664 |       int suffixStart = pos + codePlaceholder.length();
665 |       if (suffixStart != wrapper.length()) {
666 |         // Something after placeholder?
667 |         out.append(wrapper.substring(suffixStart));
668 |       }
669 |       // Make sure we always end output with a line feed.
670 |       out.append('\n');
671 | 
672 |       // If we have a source map, adjust its offsets to match
673 |       // the code WITHIN the wrapper.
674 |       if (compiler != null && compiler.getSourceMap() != null) {
675 |         compiler.getSourceMap().setWrapperPrefix(prefix);
676 |       }
677 | 
678 |     } else {
679 |       out.append(code);
680 |       out.append('\n');
681 |     }
682 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 616-698
Documentation:

Method code:
616 |     @Override
617 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
618 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
619 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
620 |       if (name == null) {
621 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
622 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
623 |           errorReporter.error(
624 |             "unnamed function statement",
625 |             sourceName,
626 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
627 | 
628 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
629 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
630 |         }
631 |         name = new Name();
632 |         name.setIdentifier("");
633 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
634 |       }
635 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
636 |       Node newName = transform(name);
637 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
638 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
639 |         // declaration.

641 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
642 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
643 |         // same line as the function keyword.
644 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
645 |             functionNode.getLp();
646 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
647 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
648 |       }
649 | 
650 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
651 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
652 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
653 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
654 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
655 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
656 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
657 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
658 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
659 |       if (fnName != null) {
660 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
661 |       } else {
662 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
663 |       }
664 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
665 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
666 | 
667 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
668 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
669 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
670 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
671 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
672 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
673 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
674 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
675 |         } else {
676 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
677 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
678 |           // (an error has already been reported).
679 |           Preconditions.checkState(
680 |               config.isIdeMode
681 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
682 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
683 |         }
684 |       }
685 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
686 | 
687 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
688 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
689 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
690 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
691 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
693 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
694 |       }
695 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
696 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
697 |      return node;
698 |     }

367 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
368 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {

373 |     }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String>,CompilerOptions,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 1289-1348
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a map of constant names to constant values from a textual
   * description of the map.
   *
   * @param definitions A list of overriding definitions for defines in
   *     the form <name>[=<val>], where <val> is a number, boolean, or
   *     single-quoted string without single quotes.
   */
Method code:
1289 |   @VisibleForTesting
1290 |   static void createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String> definitions,
1291 |       CompilerOptions options, boolean tweaks) {
1292 |     // Parse the definitions
1293 |     for (String override : definitions) {
1294 |       String[] assignment = override.split("=", 2);
1295 |       String defName = assignment[0];
1296 | 
1297 |       if (defName.length() > 0) {
1298 |         String defValue = assignment.length == 1 ? "true" : assignment[1];
1299 | 
1300 |         boolean isTrue = defValue.equals("true");
1301 |         boolean isFalse = defValue.equals("false");
1302 |         if (isTrue || isFalse) {
1303 |           if (tweaks) {
1304 |             options.setTweakToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1305 |           } else {
1306 |             options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1307 |           }
1308 |           continue;
1309 |         } else if (defValue.length() > 1
1310 |             && ((defValue.charAt(0) == '\'' &&
1311 |                 defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\'')
1312 |                 || (defValue.charAt(0) == '\"' &&
1313 |                     defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\"'))) {
1314 |           // If the value starts and ends with a single quote,
1315 |           // we assume that it's a string.
1316 |           String maybeStringVal =
1317 |               defValue.substring(1, defValue.length() - 1);
1318 |           if (maybeStringVal.indexOf(defValue.charAt(0)) == -1) {
1319 |             if (tweaks) {
1320 |               options.setTweakToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1321 |             } else {
1322 |               options.setDefineToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1323 |             }
1324 |             continue;
1325 |           }
1326 |         } else {
1327 |           try {
1328 |             double value = Double.parseDouble(defValue);
1329 |             if (tweaks) {
1330 |               options.setTweakToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1331 |             } else {
1332 |               options.setDefineToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1333 |             }
1334 |             continue;
1335 |           } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
1336 |             // do nothing, it will be caught at the end
1337 |           }
1338 |         }
1339 |       }
1340 | 
1341 |       if (tweaks) {
1342 |         throw new RuntimeException(
1343 |             "--tweak flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1344 |       }
1345 |       throw new RuntimeException(
1346 |           "--define flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1347 |     }
1348 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 242-305
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
242 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
243 |     this.options = options;
244 |     if (errorManager == null) {
245 |       if (outStream == null) {
246 |         setErrorManager(
247 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
248 |       } else {
249 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
250 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
251 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
252 |         setErrorManager(printer);
253 |       }
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
257 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
258 |       options.checkTypes = true;
259 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
260 |       options.checkTypes = false;
261 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
262 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
263 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
264 |       // parser type warnings.
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
267 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
268 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
272 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
273 |       options.setWarningLevel(
274 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
275 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
279 |       options.setWarningLevel(
280 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
281 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
282 |     }
283 | 
284 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
285 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
286 |     guards.add(
287 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
288 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
289 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
290 | 
291 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
292 | 
293 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
294 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
295 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
296 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
297 |     // error off.
298 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
299 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
300 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
301 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
305 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1113 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1114 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1115 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1116 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1117 | 
1118 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1119 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1120 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1121 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1122 | 
1123 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1124 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1125 |     }
1126 |     return typeNode;
1127 |   }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1014-1093
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1014 |   private static String strEscape(
1015 |       String s, char quote,
1016 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1017 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1018 |       String backslashEscape,
1019 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1020 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1021 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1022 |     sb.append(quote);
1023 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1024 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1025 |       switch (c) {
1026 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1027 |         case '\u000B':
1028 |           if (useSlashV) {
1029 |             sb.append("\\v");
1030 |           } else {
1031 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1032 |           }
1033 |           break;
1034 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1035 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1036 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1037 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1038 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1039 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1040 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1041 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1042 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1043 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1044 |             sb.append("\\>");
1045 |           } else {
1046 |             sb.append(c);
1047 |           }
1048 |           break;
1049 |         case '<':
1050 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1051 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1052 | 
1053 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1054 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1055 | 
1056 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1057 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1058 |             sb.append("<\\");
1059 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1060 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1061 |             sb.append("<\\");
1062 |           } else {
1063 |             sb.append(c);
1064 |           }
1065 |           break;
1066 |         default:
1067 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1068 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1069 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1070 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1071 |               sb.append(c);
1072 |             } else {
1073 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1074 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1075 |             }
1076 |           } else {
1077 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1078 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1079 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1080 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1081 |               sb.append(c);
1082 |             } else {
1083 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1084 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1085 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1086 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1087 |             }
1088 |           }
1089 |       }
1090 |     }
1091 |     sb.append(quote);
1092 |     return sb.toString();
1093 |   }

   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JSDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview jsdoc, or
   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).
   */
Method code:

341 | 
342 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
343 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;

346 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),

350 |           irNode,

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 794-807
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CommandLineRunner.processArgs(String[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 572-598
Documentation:

Method code:
572 |   private List<String> processArgs(String[] args) {
573 |     // Args4j has a different format that the old command-line parser.
574 |     // So we use some voodoo to get the args into the format that args4j
575 |     // expects.
576 |     Pattern argPattern = Pattern.compile("(--[a-zA-Z_]+)=(.*)");
577 |     Pattern quotesPattern = Pattern.compile("^['\"](.*)['\"]$");
578 |     List<String> processedArgs = Lists.newArrayList();
579 | 
580 |     for (String arg : args) {
581 |       Matcher matcher = argPattern.matcher(arg);
582 |       if (matcher.matches()) {
583 |         processedArgs.add(matcher.group(1));
584 | 
585 |         String value = matcher.group(2);
586 |         Matcher quotesMatcher = quotesPattern.matcher(value);
587 |         if (quotesMatcher.matches()) {
588 |           processedArgs.add(quotesMatcher.group(1));
589 |         } else {
590 |           processedArgs.add(value);
591 |         }
592 |       } else {
593 |         processedArgs.add(arg);
594 |       }
595 |     }
596 | 
597 |     return processedArgs;
598 |   }

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1236-1387
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1236 |   Node parseInputs() {
1237 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1238 | 
1239 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1240 |     // individual file parse trees.
1241 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1242 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1243 |     }
1244 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1245 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1246 |     }
1247 | 
1248 |     // Parse main js sources.
1249 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1250 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1251 | 
1252 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1253 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1254 | 
1255 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1256 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1257 | 
1258 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1259 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1260 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1261 |     }
1262 | 
1263 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1264 | 
1265 |     try {
1266 |       // Parse externs sources.
1267 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1268 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1269 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1270 |           return null;
1271 |         }
1272 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1273 |       }
1274 | 
1275 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1276 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1277 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1278 |       }
1279 | 
1280 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1281 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1282 | 
1283 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1284 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1285 |           !options.skipAllPasses &&
1286 |           options.closurePass) {
1287 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1288 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1289 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1290 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1291 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1292 |           }
1293 |         }
1294 | 
1295 |         try {
1296 |           inputs =
1297 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1298 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1299 |           staleInputs = true;
1300 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1301 |           report(JSError.make(
1302 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1303 | 
1304 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1305 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1306 |             return null;
1307 |           }
1308 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1309 |           report(JSError.make(
1310 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1311 | 
1312 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1313 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1314 |             return null;
1315 |           }
1316 |         }
1317 |       }
1318 | 
1319 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1320 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1321 | 
1322 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1323 |         if (n == null) {
1324 |           continue;
1325 |         }
1326 | 
1327 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1328 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1329 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1330 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1331 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1332 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1333 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1334 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1335 | 
1336 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1337 | 
1338 |             externs.add(input);
1339 |             staleInputs = true;
1340 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1341 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1342 |             staleInputs = true;
1343 |           }
1344 |         }
1345 |       }
1346 | 
1347 |       if (staleInputs) {
1348 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1349 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1350 |       }
1351 | 
1352 |       // Build the AST.
1353 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1354 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1355 |         if (n == null) {
1356 |           continue;
1357 |         }
1358 | 
1359 |         if (devMode) {
1360 |           runSanityCheck();
1361 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1362 |             return null;
1363 |           }
1364 |         }
1365 | 
1366 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1367 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1368 | 
1369 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1370 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1371 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1372 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1373 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1374 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1381 |         return null;
1382 |       }
1383 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1384 |     } finally {
1385 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1386 |     }
1387 |   }

90 |     } catch (IOException e) {

92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

===== M0195 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 181-949
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
181 |   boolean parse() {
182 |     int lineno;
183 |     int charno;
184 | 
185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
186 |     JSTypeExpression type;
187 | 
188 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
189 |     skipEOLs();
190 | 
191 |     JsDocToken token = next();
192 | 
193 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
194 | 
195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {
214 |         case ANNOTATION:
215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
217 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {
226 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
228 | 
229 |               switch (annotation) {
230 |                 case AUTHOR:
231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
234 | 
235 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     } else {
239 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
240 |                     }
241 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
242 |                   } else {
243 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
244 |                   }
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;
262 | 
263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                     } else {
269 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
270 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
271 |                     }
272 |                   }
273 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
274 |                   continue retry;
275 | 
276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
278 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 | 
282 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
289 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
290 |                   }
291 | 
292 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
293 |                   continue retry;
294 | 
295 |                 case INTERFACE:
296 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
298 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
299 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
300 |                     } else {
301 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
302 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
306 |                   continue retry;
307 | 
308 |                 case DESC:
309 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
310 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
311 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
312 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
313 |                     continue retry;
314 |                   } else {
315 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
316 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
317 | 
318 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
319 | 
320 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
321 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
322 |                     continue retry;
323 |                   }
324 | 
325 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
326 |                   String fileOverview = "";
327 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
328 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
329 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
330 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
331 | 
332 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
333 | 
334 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
335 |                   } else {
336 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
337 |                   }
338 | 
339 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
340 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
372 |                   }
373 | 
374 |                   if (type == null) {
375 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
376 |                   }
377 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
378 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
379 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
380 |                   }
381 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
382 |                   continue retry;
383 | 
384 |                 case EXPORT:
385 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
386 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
387 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
388 |                   }
389 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
390 |                   continue retry;
391 | 
392 |                 case EXPOSE:
393 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
394 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
395 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
396 |                   }
397 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
398 |                   continue retry;
399 | 
400 |                 case EXTERNS:
401 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
402 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
403 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
404 |                   }
405 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
406 |                   continue retry;
407 | 
408 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
409 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
410 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
411 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
412 |                   }
413 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
414 |                   continue retry;
415 | 
416 |                 case EXTENDS:
417 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();
419 |                   token = next();
420 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
421 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
422 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
423 | 
424 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
425 |                     token = next();
426 |                     matchingRc = true;
427 |                   }
428 | 
429 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
430 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
431 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
432 | 
433 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
434 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
435 | 
436 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
437 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
438 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
439 |                     }
440 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
441 | 
442 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
443 |                       // record the extended type, check later
444 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
445 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
446 |                     } else {
447 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
448 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
449 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
450 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
451 |                             lineno, charno);
452 |                       }
453 |                     }
454 |                     token = next();
455 |                     if (matchingRc) {
456 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
457 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
458 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
459 |                       }
460 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
461 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
462 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
463 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
464 |                     }
465 |                   } else {
466 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
467 |                   }
468 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
469 |                   continue retry;
470 | 
471 |                 case HIDDEN:
472 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
473 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }
476 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
477 |                   continue retry;
478 | 
479 |                 case LENDS:
480 |                   skipEOLs();
481 | 
482 |                   matchingRc = false;
483 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
484 |                     token = next();
485 |                     matchingRc = true;
486 |                   }
487 | 
488 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
489 |                     token = next();
490 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
491 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
492 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                     }
494 |                   } else {
495 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
496 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
497 |                   }
498 | 
499 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
500 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
501 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
502 |                   }
503 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
504 |                   continue retry;
505 | 
506 |                 case MEANING:
507 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
508 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
509 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
510 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
511 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
512 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
513 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
514 |                   }
515 |                   continue retry;
516 | 
517 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
518 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
519 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
520 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
521 |                   }
522 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
523 |                   continue retry;
524 | 
525 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
526 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
527 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
528 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
529 |                   }
530 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
531 |                   continue retry;
532 | 
533 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
534 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
535 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
536 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
537 |                   }
538 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
539 |                   continue retry;
540 | 
541 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
542 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
543 |                   continue retry;
544 | 
545 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
546 |                 case OVERRIDE:
547 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
548 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
549 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
550 |                   }
551 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
552 |                   continue retry;
553 | 
554 |                 case THROWS:
555 |                   skipEOLs();
556 |                   token = next();
557 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
558 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
559 |                   type = null;
560 | 
561 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
562 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
563 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
564 | 
565 |                     if (type == null) {
566 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
567 |                       // recovering parsing
568 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
569 |                       continue retry;
570 |                     }
571 |                   }
572 | 
573 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
574 |                   token = current();
575 | 
576 |                   // Save the throw type.
577 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
578 | 
579 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
580 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
581 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
582 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
583 | 
584 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
585 | 
586 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
587 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
591 |                   } else {
592 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
593 |                   }
594 |                   continue retry;
595 | 
596 |                 case PARAM:
597 |                   skipEOLs();
598 |                   token = next();
599 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
600 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
601 |                   type = null;
602 | 
603 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
604 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
605 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
606 | 
607 |                     if (type == null) {
608 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
609 |                       // recovering parsing
610 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
611 |                       continue retry;
612 |                     }
613 |                     skipEOLs();
614 |                     token = next();
615 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
617 |                   }
618 | 
619 |                   String name = null;
620 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
621 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
622 |                     token = next();
623 |                   }
624 | 
625 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
626 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
627 |                         lineno, charno);
628 |                   } else {
629 |                     name = stream.getString();
630 | 
631 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
632 |                       token = next();
633 | 
634 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
635 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
636 |                       // system.
637 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
638 |                         token = next();
639 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
640 |                           token = next();
641 |                         }
642 |                       }
643 | 
644 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
645 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
646 |                       } else if (type != null) {
647 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
648 |                         // already.
649 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
650 |                       }
651 |                     }
652 | 
653 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
654 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
655 |                     // for handling properties of params.
656 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
657 |                       name = null;
658 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
659 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
660 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
661 |                             lineno, charno);
662 |                       } else {
663 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
664 |                             lineno, charno);
665 |                       }
666 |                     }
667 |                   }
668 | 
669 |                   if (name == null) {
670 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
671 |                     continue retry;
672 |                   }
673 | 
674 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
675 | 
676 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
677 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
678 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
679 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
680 | 
681 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
682 | 
683 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
684 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
685 |                           paramDescription);
686 |                     }
687 | 
688 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
689 |                   } else {
690 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
691 |                   }
692 |                   continue retry;
693 | 
694 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
695 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
696 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
697 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
698 |                   }
699 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
700 |                   continue retry;
701 | 
702 |                 case PRIVATE:
703 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
704 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
705 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
706 |                   }
707 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
708 |                   continue retry;
709 | 
710 |                 case PROTECTED:
711 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
712 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
713 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
714 |                   }
715 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case PUBLIC:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
720 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
727 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
728 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
729 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
730 |                   }
731 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
732 |                   continue retry;
733 | 
734 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
735 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
736 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
737 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
738 |                   }
739 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
740 |                   continue retry;
741 | 
742 |                 case MODIFIES:
743 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
747 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
748 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
749 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
750 |                   }
751 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
752 |                   continue retry;
753 | 
754 |                 case SEE:
755 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
756 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
757 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
758 | 
759 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
760 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
761 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
762 |                     } else {
763 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
764 |                     }
765 | 
766 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
767 |                   } else {
768 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
769 |                   }
770 |                   continue retry;
771 | 
772 |                 case SUPPRESS:
773 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
774 |                   continue retry;
775 | 
776 |                 case TEMPLATE:
777 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
778 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
779 | 
780 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
781 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
782 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
783 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
784 |                       templateTypeName)) {
785 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
786 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
787 |                   }
788 | 
789 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
790 |                   continue retry;
791 | 
792 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
793 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
794 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
795 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
796 |                   }
797 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
798 |                   continue retry;
799 | 
800 |                 case VERSION:
801 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
802 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
803 | 
804 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
805 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
806 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
807 |                   } else {
808 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
809 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
810 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
811 |                     }
812 |                   }
813 | 
814 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
815 |                   continue retry;
816 | 
817 |                 case DEFINE:
818 |                 case RETURN:
819 |                 case THIS:
820 |                 case TYPE:
821 |                 case TYPEDEF:
822 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
823 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
824 | 
825 |                   Node typeNode = null;
826 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
827 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
828 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
829 |                     // it as the unknown type.
830 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
831 |                   } else {
832 |                     skipEOLs();
833 |                     token = next();
834 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
835 |                   }
836 | 
837 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
838 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
839 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
840 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
841 |                     }
842 |                   }
843 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
844 | 
845 |                   if (type == null) {
846 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
847 |                     // recovering parsing
848 |                   } else {
849 |                     switch (annotation) {
850 |                       case DEFINE:
851 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
852 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
853 |                               lineno, charno);
854 |                         }
855 |                         break;
856 | 
857 |                       case RETURN:
858 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
859 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
860 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
861 |                           break;
862 |                         }
863 | 
864 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
865 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
866 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
867 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
868 | 
869 |                           String returnDescription =
870 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
871 | 
872 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
873 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
874 |                                 returnDescription);
875 |                           }
876 | 
877 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
878 |                         } else {
879 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
880 |                         }
881 |                         continue retry;
882 | 
883 |                       case THIS:
884 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
885 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
886 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
887 |                         }
888 |                         break;
889 | 
890 |                       case TYPE:
891 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
892 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
893 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
894 |                         }
895 |                         break;
896 | 
897 |                       case TYPEDEF:
898 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
899 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
900 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
901 |                         }
902 |                         break;
903 |                     }
904 |                   }
905 | 
906 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
907 |                   continue retry;
908 |               }
909 |             }
910 |           }
911 |           break;
912 | 
913 |         case EOC:
914 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
915 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
916 |           }
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return true;
919 | 
920 |         case EOF:
921 |           // discard any accumulated information
922 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
923 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
924 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
925 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
926 |           return false;
927 | 
928 |         case EOL:
929 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
930 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
931 |           }
932 |           token = next();
933 |           continue retry;
934 | 
935 |         default:
936 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
937 |             token = next();
938 |             continue retry;
939 |           } else {
940 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
941 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
942 |             continue retry;
943 |           }
944 |       }
945 | 
946 |       // next token
947 |       token = next();
948 |     }
949 |   }

1049 |     @Override
1050 |     Node processVariableDeclaration(VariableDeclaration declarationNode) {
1051 |       if (!config.acceptConstKeyword && declarationNode.getType() ==
1052 |           com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CONST) {
1053 |         processIllegalToken(declarationNode);
1054 |       }
1055 | 
1056 |       Node node = newNode(Token.VAR);
1057 |       for (VariableInitializer child : declarationNode.getVariables()) {
1058 |         node.addChildToBack(transform(child));
1059 |       }
1060 |       return node;
1061 |     }

/**
     * A javascript module specification. The format is
     * <name>:<num-js-files>[:[<dep>,...][:]]]. Module names must be
     * unique. Each dep is the name of a module that this module
     * depends on. Modules must be listed in dependency order, and js
     * source files must be listed in the corresponding order. Where
     * --module flags occur in relation to --js flags is unimportant
     */
