===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 223-245
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 | 
236 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }
245 |     }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-730
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         add("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         add("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         add("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n);
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       default:
726 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
727 |     }
728 | 
729 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
730 |   }

464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 |

473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a

479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }

712 |   protected CompilerOptions createOptions() {

715 |     CompilationLevel level = flags.compilation_level;
716 |     level.setOptionsForCompilationLevel(options);

718 |       level.setDebugOptionsForCompilationLevel(options);
719 |     }

722 |       options.setGenerateExports(flags.generate_exports);
723 |     }
724 | 
725 |     WarningLevel wLevel = flags.warning_level;
726 |     wLevel.setOptionsForWarningLevel(options);
727 |     for (FormattingOption formattingOption : flags.formatting) {
728 |       formattingOption.applyToOptions(options);
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     options.closurePass = flags.process_closure_primitives;
732 | 
733 |     options.jqueryPass = flags.process_jquery_primitives &&
734 |         CompilationLevel.ADVANCED_OPTIMIZATIONS == level;
735 | 
736 |     if (flags.process_jquery_primitives) {
737 |       options.setCodingConvention(new JqueryCodingConvention());
738 |     }
739 |

741 |       try {
742 |         options.messageBundle = new XtbMessageBundle(
743 |             new FileInputStream(flags.translationsFile),
744 |             flags.translationsProject);
745 |       } catch (IOException e) {
746 |         throw new RuntimeException("Reading XTB file", e);
747 |       }
748 |     } else if (CompilationLevel.ADVANCED_OPTIMIZATIONS == level) {
749 |       // In SIMPLE or WHITESPACE mode, if the user hasn't specified a
750 |       // translations file, they might reasonably try to write their own
751 |       // implementation of goog.getMsg that makes the substitution at
752 |       // run-time.
753 |       //
754 |       // In ADVANCED mode, goog.getMsg is going to be renamed anyway,
755 |       // so we might as well inline it.
756 |       options.messageBundle = new EmptyMessageBundle();
757 |     }
758 |

760 |   }

182 |         }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 878-892
Documentation:
// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
878 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
879 |     int count = 0;
880 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
881 |       count++;
882 |     }
883 | 
884 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
885 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
886 |       count--;
887 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
888 |     }
889 | 
890 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
891 |     return keys;
892 |   }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 660-705
Documentation:

Method code:
660 |   private void compileInternal() {
661 |     setProgress(0.0);
662 |     parse();
663 |     // 15 percent of the work is assumed to be for parsing (based on some
664 |     // minimal analysis on big JS projects, of course this depends on options)
665 |     setProgress(0.15);
666 |     if (hasErrors()) {
667 |       return;
668 |     }
669 | 
670 |     if (!precheck()) {
671 |       return;
672 |     }
673 | 
674 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
675 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
676 |       check();
677 |       return;
678 |     }
679 | 
680 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
681 |       check();
682 |       if (hasErrors()) {
683 |         return;
684 |       }
685 | 
686 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
687 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
688 |         externExports();
689 |       }
690 | 
691 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
692 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
693 |         optimize();
694 |       }
695 |     }
696 | 
697 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
698 |       recordFunctionInformation();
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
702 |       runSanityCheck();
703 |     }
704 |     setProgress(1.0);
705 |   }

===== M0262 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CommandLineRunner.Flags.getJsFiles()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 421-427
Documentation:
/**
     * Users may specify JS inputs via the legacy {@code --js} option, as well
     * as via additional arguments to the Closure Compiler. For example, it is
     * convenient to leverage the additional arguments feature when using the
     * Closure Compiler in combination with {@code find} and {@code xargs}:
     * <pre>
     * find MY_JS_SRC_DIR -name '*.js' \
     *     | xargs java -jar compiler.jar --manage_closure_dependencies
     * </pre>
     * The {@code find} command will produce a list of '*.js' source files in
     * the {@code MY_JS_SRC_DIR} directory while {@code xargs} will convert them
     * to a single, space-delimited set of arguments that are appended to the
     * {@code java} command to run the Compiler.
     * <p>
     * Note that it is important to use the
     * {@code --manage_closure_dependencies} option in this case because the
     * order produced by {@code find} is unlikely to be sorted correctly with
     * respect to {@code goog.provide()} and {@code goog.requires()}.
     */
Method code:
421 |     List<String> getJsFiles() {
422 |       List<String> allJsInputs = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(
423 |           js.size() + arguments.size());
424 |       allJsInputs.addAll(js);
425 |       allJsInputs.addAll(arguments);
426 |       return allJsInputs;
427 |     }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 489-506
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1095-1115
Documentation:

Method code:
1095 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1096 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1097 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1098 |       return s;
1099 |     }
1100 | 
1101 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1102 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1103 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1104 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1105 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1106 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1107 |       // crazy.
1108 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1109 |         sb.append(c);
1110 |       } else {
1111 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1112 |       }
1113 |     }
1114 |     return sb.toString();
1115 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1764-1799
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1764 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1765 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1766 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1767 |     }
1768 | 
1769 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1770 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1771 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1772 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1773 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1774 |       skipEOLs();
1775 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1776 |         next();
1777 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1778 |       }
1779 |     }
1780 | 
1781 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1782 | 
1783 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1784 |       next();
1785 |       skipEOLs();
1786 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1787 |       if (memberType != null) {
1788 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1789 | 
1790 |         skipEOLs();
1791 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1792 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1793 |         }
1794 | 
1795 |         next();
1796 |       }
1797 |     }
1798 |     return typeNameNode;
1799 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 684-720
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
684 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
685 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  boolean preferEndOfFileBreak,
686 |                                  int lineLengthThreshold,
687 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
688 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
689 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
690 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
691 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
692 | 
693 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
694 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
695 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
696 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
697 |             lineBreak, preferEndOfFileBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
698 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
699 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
700 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
701 |     CodeGenerator cg =
702 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
703 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
704 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
705 | 
706 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
707 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
708 |     }
709 | 
710 |     cg.add(root);
711 |     mcp.endFile();
712 | 
713 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
714 | 
715 |     if (createSourceMap) {
716 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     return code;
720 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.outputManifestOrBundle(List<String>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 1371-1413
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes the manifest or bundle of all compiler input files that survived
   * manage_closure_dependencies, if requested.
   */
Method code:
1371 |   private void outputManifestOrBundle(List<String> outputFiles,
1372 |       boolean isManifest) throws IOException {
1373 |     if (outputFiles.isEmpty()) {
1374 |       return;
1375 |     }
1376 | 
1377 |     for (String output : outputFiles) {
1378 |       if (output.isEmpty()) {
1379 |         continue;
1380 |       }
1381 | 
1382 |       if (shouldGenerateOutputPerModule(output)) {
1383 |         // Generate per-module manifests or bundles
1384 |         JSModuleGraph graph = compiler.getDegenerateModuleGraph();
1385 |         Iterable<JSModule> modules = graph.getAllModules();
1386 |         for (JSModule module : modules) {
1387 |           Writer out = fileNameToOutputWriter2(
1388 |               expandCommandLinePath(output, module));
1389 |           if (isManifest) {
1390 |             printManifestTo(module.getInputs(), out);
1391 |           } else {
1392 |             printBundleTo(module.getInputs(), out);
1393 |           }
1394 |           out.close();
1395 |         }
1396 |       } else {
1397 |         // Generate a single file manifest or bundle.
1398 |         Writer out = fileNameToOutputWriter2(
1399 |             expandCommandLinePath(output, null));
1400 |         if (config.module.isEmpty()) {
1401 |           if (isManifest) {
1402 |             printManifestTo(compiler.getInputsInOrder(), out);
1403 |           } else {
1404 |             printBundleTo(compiler.getInputsInOrder(), out);
1405 |           }
1406 |         } else {
1407 |           printModuleGraphManifestOrBundleTo(
1408 |               compiler.getDegenerateModuleGraph(), out, isManifest);
1409 |         }
1410 |         out.close();
1411 |       }
1412 |     }
1413 |   }

===== M0328 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 965-992
Documentation:

Method code:
965 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
966 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
967 | 
968 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
969 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
970 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
971 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
972 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
973 |       }
974 |     }
975 | 
976 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
977 |     char quote;
978 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
979 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
980 |       quote = '\'';
981 |       doublequote = "\"";
982 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
983 |     } else {
984 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
985 |       quote = '\"';
986 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
987 |       singlequote = "\'";
988 |     }
989 | 
990 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
991 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
992 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1635 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1636 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1637 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1638 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1639 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1640 |         next();
1641 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1642 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1643 |           next();
1644 |         }
1645 |         skipEOLs();
1646 |         token = next();
1647 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1648 |       }
1649 |     }
1650 |     return typeExpr;
1651 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.toSource(CodeBuilder,int,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1628-1690
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes out js code from a root node. If printing input delimiters, this
   * method will attach a comment to the start of the text indicating which
   * input the output derived from. If there were any preserve annotations
   * within the root's source, they will also be printed in a block comment
   * at the beginning of the output.
   */
Method code:
1628 |   public void toSource(final CodeBuilder cb,
1629 |                        final int inputSeqNum,
1630 |                        final Node root) {
1631 |     runInCompilerThread(new Callable<Void>() {
1632 |       @Override
1633 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1634 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1635 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1636 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1637 |           }
1638 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.isScript());
1639 | 
1640 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1641 | 
1642 |           String inputName = root.getInputId().getIdName();
1643 |           String sourceName = root.getSourceFileName();
1644 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1645 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1646 | 
1647 |           delimiter = delimiter
1648 |               .replaceAll("%name%", Matcher.quoteReplacement(inputName))
1649 |               .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1650 | 
1651 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1652 |             .append("\n");
1653 |         }
1654 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1655 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1656 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1657 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1658 |             .append("*/\n");
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1662 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1663 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1664 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1665 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1666 |         }
1667 | 
1668 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1669 |         // for the first input file
1670 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap, inputSeqNum == 0);
1671 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1672 |           cb.append(code);
1673 | 
1674 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1675 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1676 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1677 |           int length = code.length();
1678 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1679 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1680 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1681 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1682 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1683 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1684 |             cb.append(";");
1685 |           }
1686 |         }
1687 |         return null;
1688 |       }
1689 |     });
1690 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 |

175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&

179 |       }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1684-1725
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1684 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1685 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1686 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1687 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1688 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1689 |       // {?} - right curly
1690 |       // {?=} - equals
1691 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1692 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1693 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1694 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1695 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1696 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1697 |       token = next();
1698 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1699 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1700 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1701 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1702 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1703 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1704 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1705 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1706 |       }
1707 | 
1708 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1709 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1710 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1711 |     } else {
1712 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1713 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1714 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1715 |           next();
1716 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1717 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1718 |           next();
1719 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1720 |         }
1721 |       }
1722 | 
1723 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1724 |     }
1725 |   }

===== M0368 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 399-412
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
399 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
400 |       Node ret;
401 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
402 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
403 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
404 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       } else {
407 |         ret = transform(n);
408 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
409 |       }
410 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
411 |       return ret;
412 |     }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 460-475
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
460 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
461 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
462 |       Set<String> directives = null;
463 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
464 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
465 |         if (directives == null) {
466 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
467 |         } else {
468 |           directives.add(directive);
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       if (directives != null) {
473 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
474 |       }
475 |     }

/**

   * not an operator

2123 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2124 |     switch (operator) {
2125 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2126 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2127 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2128 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";

2145 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2146 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1555
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1526 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1527 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1529 |         return false;
1530 |       }
1531 | 
1532 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1533 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1534 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1535 |         return false;
1536 |       }
1537 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1538 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1539 |         return false;
1540 |       }
1541 |     }
1542 | 
1543 |     if (recurse) {
1544 |       Node n, n2;
1545 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1546 |            n != null;
1547 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1548 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1549 |           return false;
1550 |         }
1551 |       }
1552 |     }
1553 | 
1554 |     return true;
1555 |   }

449 | 
450 |         if (param == null) {
451 |           setter.addValue(true);
452 |           return 0;
453 |         } else {
454 |           String lowerParam = param.toLowerCase();
455 |           if (TRUES.contains(lowerParam)) {
456 |             setter.addValue(true);
457 |           } else if (FALSES.contains(lowerParam)) {
458 |             setter.addValue(false);
459 |           } else {
460 |             setter.addValue(true);
461 |             return 0;
462 |           }
463 |           return 1;
464 |         }
465 |       }

1741 |       // Adjust the line and column information for the new text.
1742 |       int index = -1;
1743 |       int lastIndex = index;
1744 |       while ((index = str.indexOf('\n', index + 1)) >= 0) {
1745 |         ++lineCount;
1746 |         lastIndex = index;
1747 |       }

1752 |       } else {

1754 |       }
1755 |

1757 |     }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
466 |   private static boolean mayBeStatement(Node n) {
467 |     switch (n.getType()) {
468 |       case Token.EMPTY:
469 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
470 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
471 |         // contexts
472 |         return true;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.BLOCK:
475 |       case Token.BREAK:
476 |       case Token.CONST:
477 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
478 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
479 |       case Token.DO:
480 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
481 |       case Token.FOR:
482 |       case Token.IF:
483 |       case Token.LABEL:
484 |       case Token.RETURN:
485 |       case Token.SWITCH:
486 |       case Token.THROW:
487 |       case Token.TRY:
488 |       case Token.VAR:
489 |       case Token.WHILE:
490 |       case Token.WITH:
491 |         return true;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         return false;
495 |     }
496 |   }

===== M0448 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

1259 |       }

1264 |         compiler.getPropertyMap().save(propertyMapOutputPath);

1632 |       @Override
1633 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1634 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1635 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1636 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1637 |           }
1638 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.isScript());
1639 | 
1640 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1641 | 
1642 |           String inputName = root.getInputId().getIdName();
1643 |           String sourceName = root.getSourceFileName();
1644 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1645 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1646 | 
1647 |           delimiter = delimiter
1648 |               .replaceAll("%name%", Matcher.quoteReplacement(inputName))
1649 |               .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1650 | 
1651 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1652 |             .append("\n");
1653 |         }
1654 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1655 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1656 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1657 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1658 |             .append("*/\n");
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1662 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1663 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1664 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1665 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1666 |         }
1667 | 
1668 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1669 |         // for the first input file
1670 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap, inputSeqNum == 0);
1671 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1672 |           cb.append(code);
1673 | 
1674 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1675 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1676 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1677 |           int length = code.length();
1678 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1679 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1680 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1681 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1682 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1683 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1684 |             cb.append(";");
1685 |           }
1686 |         }
1687 |         return null;
1688 |       }

===== M0490 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0491 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.JSDocInfo.declareParam(JSTypeExpression,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/JSDocInfo.java
Original range: 889-900
Documentation:
/**
   * Declares a parameter. Parameters are described using the {@code @param}
   * annotation.
   *
   * @param jsType the parameter's type, it may be {@code null} when the
   *     {@code @param} annotation did not specify a type.
   * @param parameter the parameter's name
   */
Method code:
889 |   boolean declareParam(JSTypeExpression jsType, String parameter) {
890 |     lazyInitInfo();
891 |     if (info.parameters == null) {
892 |       info.parameters = new LinkedHashMap<String, JSTypeExpression>();
893 |     }

===== M0494 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2399-2409
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2399 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2400 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2401 |     int len = s.length();
2402 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2403 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2404 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2405 |         return false;
2406 |       }
2407 |     }
2408 |     return true;
2409 |   }

===== M0497 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1570-1605
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1570 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1571 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1572 | 
1573 |     skipEOLs();
1574 | 
1575 |     boolean restArg = false;
1576 |     token = next();
1577 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1578 |       token = next();
1579 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1580 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1581 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1582 |       }
1583 |       restArg = true;
1584 |     }
1585 | 
1586 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1587 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1588 |       skipEOLs();
1589 |       if (restArg) {
1590 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1591 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1592 |         next();
1593 |         skipEOLs();
1594 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1595 |       }
1596 | 
1597 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1598 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1599 |       } else {
1600 |         next();
1601 |       }
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     return typeNode;
1605 |   }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1731-1757
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1731 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1732 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1733 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1734 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1735 |       skipEOLs();
1736 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1737 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1738 |       skipEOLs();
1739 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1740 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1741 |       skipEOLs();
1742 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1743 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1744 |       String string = stream.getString();
1745 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1746 |         skipEOLs();
1747 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1748 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1749 |         return newStringNode(string);
1750 |       } else {
1751 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1752 |       }
1753 |     }
1754 | 
1755 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1756 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1757 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
454 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
455 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
456 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
457 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
458 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
459 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {

465 |         inputs.add(input);
466 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |     return inputs;
470 |   }

   */

1501 |   private Map<String, String> constructRootRelativePathsMap() {
