===== M0531 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.processResults(Result,List<JSModule>,B)

Original range: 756-878

/**
   * Processes the results of the compile job, and returns an error code.
   */
Method code:
756 |   int processResults(Result result, List<JSModule> modules, B options)
757 |        throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
758 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
759 |       return 0;
760 |     }
761 | 
762 |     if (config.printPassGraph) {
763 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
764 |         return 1;
765 |       } else {
766 |         jsOutput.append(
767 |             DotFormatter.toDot(compiler.getPassConfig().getPassGraph()));
768 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
769 |         return 0;
770 |       }
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (config.printAst) {
774 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
775 |         return 1;
776 |       } else {
777 |         ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = compiler.computeCFG();
778 |         DotFormatter.appendDot(
779 |             compiler.getRoot().getLastChild(), cfg, jsOutput);
780 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
781 |         return 0;
782 |       }
783 |     }
784 | 
785 |     if (config.printTree) {
786 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
787 |         jsOutput.append("Code contains errors; no tree was generated.\n");
788 |         return 1;
789 |       } else {
790 |         compiler.getRoot().appendStringTree(jsOutput);
791 |         jsOutput.append("\n");
792 |         return 0;
793 |       }
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     rootRelativePathsMap = constructRootRelativePathsMap();
797 | 
798 |     if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
799 |       // Output the manifest and bundle files if requested.
800 |       outputManifest();
801 |       outputBundle();
802 |       return 0;
803 |     } else if (result.success) {
804 |       if (modules == null) {
805 |         writeOutput(
806 |             jsOutput, compiler, compiler.toSource(), config.outputWrapper,
807 |             OUTPUT_WRAPPER_MARKER);
808 | 
809 |         // Output the source map if requested.
810 |         outputSourceMap(options, config.jsOutputFile);
811 |       } else {
812 |         parsedModuleWrappers = parseModuleWrappers(
813 |             config.moduleWrapper, modules);
814 |         maybeCreateDirsForPath(config.moduleOutputPathPrefix);
815 | 
816 |         // If the source map path is in fact a pattern for each
817 |         // module, create a stream per-module. Otherwise, create
818 |         // a single source map.
819 |         Writer mapOut = null;
820 | 
821 |         if (!shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options)) {
822 |           mapOut = fileNameToOutputWriter2(expandSourceMapPath(options, null));
823 |         }
824 | 
825 |         for (JSModule m : modules) {
826 |           if (shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options)) {
827 |             mapOut = fileNameToOutputWriter2(expandSourceMapPath(options, m));
828 |           }
829 | 
830 |           Writer writer =
831 |               fileNameToLegacyOutputWriter(getModuleOutputFileName(m));
832 | 
833 |           if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
834 |             compiler.getSourceMap().reset();
835 |           }
836 | 
837 |           writeModuleOutput(writer, m);
838 | 
839 |           if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
840 |             compiler.getSourceMap().appendTo(mapOut, m.getName());
841 |           }
842 | 
843 |           writer.close();
844 | 
845 |           if (shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options) && mapOut != null) {
846 |             mapOut.close();
847 |             mapOut = null;
848 |           }
849 |         }
850 | 
851 |         if (mapOut != null) {
852 |           mapOut.close();
853 |         }
854 |       }
855 | 
856 |       // Output the externs if required.
857 |       if (options.externExportsPath != null) {
858 |         Writer eeOut =
859 |             openExternExportsStream(options, config.jsOutputFile);
860 |         eeOut.append(result.externExport);
861 |         eeOut.close();
862 |       }
863 | 
864 |       // Output the variable and property name maps if requested.
865 |       outputNameMaps(options);
866 | 
867 |       // Output the manifest and bundle files if requested.
868 |       outputManifest();
869 |       outputBundle();
870 | 
871 |       if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
872 |         outputTracerReport();
873 |       }
874 |     }
875 | 
876 |     // return 0 if no errors, the error count otherwise
877 |     return Math.min(result.errors.length, 0x7f);
878 |   }

Original range: 1514-1535

/**
   * Converts the main parse tree back to js code.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   public String toSource() {
1515 |     return runInCompilerThread(new Callable<String>() {
1516 |       @Override
1517 |       public String call() throws Exception {
1518 |         Tracer tracer = newTracer("toSource");
1519 |         try {
1520 |           CodeBuilder cb = new CodeBuilder();
1521 |           if (jsRoot != null) {
1522 |             int i = 0;
1523 |             for (Node scriptNode = jsRoot.getFirstChild();
1524 |                  scriptNode != null;
1525 |                  scriptNode = scriptNode.getNext()) {
1526 |               toSource(cb, i++, scriptNode);
1527 |             }
1528 |           }
1529 |           return cb.toString();
1530 |         } finally {
1531 |           stopTracer(tracer, "toSource");
1532 |         }
1533 |       }
1534 |     });
1535 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DependencyOptions.setMoocherDropping(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DependencyOptions.java
Original range: 95-98
Documentation:
/**
   * Enables or disables moocher dropping mode.
   *
   * A 'moocher' is a file that does not provide any symbols (though they
   * may require symbols). This is usually because they don't want to
   * tie themselves to a particular dependency system (e.g., Closure's
   * goog.provide, CommonJS modules). So they rely on other people to
   * manage dependencies on them.
   *
   * If true, we drop these files when we prune dependencies.
   * If false, we always keep these files an anything they depend on.
   * The default is false.
   *
   * Notice that this option only makes sense if dependency pruning is on,
   * and a set of entry points is specified.
   *
   * @return this for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   public DependencyOptions setMoocherDropping(boolean enabled) {
96 |     this.dropMoochers = enabled;
97 |     return this;
98 |   }

===== M0565 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 898-925
Documentation:

Method code:
898 |     @Override
899 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
900 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
901 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
902 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
903 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
904 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
905 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
906 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
907 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
908 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
909 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
910 |         //
911 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
912 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
913 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
914 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
915 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
916 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
917 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
918 |             (sourceString.substring(
919 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
920 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
921 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
922 |         }
923 |       }
924 |       return n;
925 |     }

===== M0570 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2345-2361
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2345 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2346 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2347 |     int c;
2348 |     while (true) {
2349 |       c = stream.getChar();
2350 |       if (c == ' ') {
2351 |         continue;
2352 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2353 |         matchedLc = true;
2354 |         break;
2355 |       } else {
2356 |         break;
2357 |       }
2358 |     }
2359 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2360 |     return matchedLc;
2361 |   }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 606-658
Documentation:

Method code:
606 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
607 |   static <T> T runCallable(
608 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
609 | 
610 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
611 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
612 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
613 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
614 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
615 | 
616 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
617 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
618 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
619 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
620 |       @Override
621 |       public void run() {
622 |         try {
623 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
624 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
625 |           }
626 |           result[0] = callable.call();
627 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
628 |           exception[0] = e;
629 |         } finally {
630 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
631 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
632 |           }
633 |         }
634 |       }
635 |     };
636 | 
637 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
638 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
639 |       th.start();
640 |       while (true) {
641 |         try {
642 |           th.join();
643 |           break;
644 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
645 |           // ignore
646 |         }
647 |       }
648 |     } else {
649 |       runnable.run();
650 |     }
651 | 
652 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
653 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
654 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     return (T) result[0];
658 |   }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.doRun()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 705-751
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses command-line arguments and runs the compiler.
   *
   * @return system exit status
   */
Method code:
705 |   protected int doRun() throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
706 |     Compiler.setLoggingLevel(Level.parse(config.loggingLevel));
707 | 
708 |     List<SourceFile> externs = createExterns();
709 | 
710 |     compiler = createCompiler();
711 |     B options = createOptions();
712 | 
713 |     List<JSModule> modules = null;
714 |     Result result = null;
715 | 
716 |     setRunOptions(options);
717 | 
718 |     boolean writeOutputToFile = !config.jsOutputFile.isEmpty();
719 |     if (writeOutputToFile) {
720 |       jsOutput = fileNameToLegacyOutputWriter(config.jsOutputFile);
721 |     } else if (jsOutput instanceof OutputStream) {
722 |       jsOutput = streamToLegacyOutputWriter((OutputStream) jsOutput);
723 |     }
724 | 
725 |     List<String> jsFiles = config.js;
726 |     List<String> moduleSpecs = config.module;
727 |     if (!moduleSpecs.isEmpty()) {
728 |       modules = createJsModules(moduleSpecs, jsFiles);
729 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
730 |         compiler.initModules(externs, modules, options);
731 |       } else {
732 |         result = compiler.compileModules(externs, modules, options);
733 |       }
734 |     } else {
735 |       List<SourceFile> inputs = createSourceInputs(jsFiles);
736 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
737 |         compiler.init(externs, inputs, options);
738 |       } else {
739 |         result = compiler.compile(externs, inputs, options);
740 |       }
741 |     }
742 | 
743 |     int errCode = processResults(result, modules, options);
744 |     // Flush the output if we are writing to a file.
745 |     // We can't close yet, because we may need to write phase ordering
746 |     // info to it later.
747 |     if (jsOutput instanceof Flushable) {
748 |       ((Flushable) jsOutput).flush();
749 |     }
750 |     return errCode;
751 |   }
